-{"content": "# Sports Federation Public Site\n\nPublic website coverage for tournaments, now integrated into the main\nTournaments navigation instead of a separate Competitions surface.\n\n## Purpose\n\nThis module turns published tournaments into a coherent public product for fans,\nclubs, and media. It provides a tournament hub, overview pages, schedule and\nresults pages, standings snapshots, team profile pages, JSON feed access, and\ncalendar export while keeping older competition URLs working.\n\n## Dependencies\n\n| Module | Reason |\n|--------|--------|\n| `website` | Odoo website framework |\n| `sports_federation_portal` | Main tournament website hub and navigation |\n| `sports_federation_tournament` | Tournaments, matches, participants |\n| `sports_federation_standings` | Standings data |\n| `sports_federation_venues` | Venue information |\n| `sports_federation_result_control` | Approved result visibility |\n\n## Model extensions\n\n### `federation.tournament`\n\n| Field | Type | Description |\n|-------|------|-------------|\n| `website_published` | Boolean | Enables public visibility |\n| `public_slug` | Char | Legacy public slug retained for tournament feed and redirect compatibility |\n| `public_description` | Html | Long-form website description |\n| `public_featured` | Boolean | Marks the tournament for featured hub sections |\n| `public_editorial_summary` | Text | Short editorial summary for cards and overview layouts |\n| `public_pinned_announcement` | Text | Announcement banner content for the overview page |\n| `public_hero_image` | Binary | Hero image for the public overview page |\n| `show_public_results` | Boolean | Enables the public results page |\n| `show_public_standings` | Boolean | Enables the public standings page |\n\nKey helper methods expose canonical public URLs, published tournament queries,\nteam/result/standings/schedule datasets, JSON feed payloads, and ICS calendar\nexport content.\n\n### `federation.team`\n\n| Field | Type | Description |\n|-------|------|-------------|\n| `public_slug` | Char | Canonical public slug used in `/teams/<slug>` |\n\nTeam helpers provide stable team profile URLs and public tournament links.\nTheir public slug resolver accepts an extra publication domain so controller\nlookups can fail closed before rendering profile, follow, feed, or calendar\nsurfaces.\n\n### `federation.season`\n\n| Field | Type | Description |\n|-------|------|-------------|\n| `website_published` | Boolean | Enables season-wide discovery on the website |\n| `public_slug` | Char | Canonical public slug used in `/seasons/<slug>` |\n| `public_summary` | Text | Short summary used on season discovery pages |\n| `public_description` | Html | Long-form season landing page content |\n\nSeason helpers provide canonical season URLs, featured and recent tournament\nqueries, editorial aggregation for season landing pages, and fail-closed public\nslug resolution for published-season routes.\n\n### `federation.public.editorial.item`\n\n| Field | Type | Description |\n|-------|------|-------------|\n| `publication_state` | Selection | draft / scheduled / published / archived |\n| `content_type` | Selection | highlight / announcement / update |\n| `publish_start` / `publish_end` | Datetime | Optional publication window |\n| `season_id` / `tournament_id` / `team_id` | Many2one | Public anchor for the item |\n| `summary` | Text | Card-friendly editorial summary |\n| `body_html` | Html | Long-form editorial content |\n\nEditorial items let operators schedule season-, tournament-, or team-linked\nhighlights without editing website templates. Scheduling requires a publish\nstart while the item is still in draft; items can publish from draft or\nscheduled, archive from scheduled or published, and reset from scheduled or\narchived back to draft.\n\n### `federation.standing`\n\n| Field | Type | Description |\n|-------|------|-------------|\n| `website_published` | Boolean | Allows standings publication |\n| `public_title` | Char | Public-facing table title |\n\n## Controllers\n\n### `PublicTournamentHubController`\n\nCanonical public routes use one namespace only:\n\n| Route | Auth | Description |\n|-------|------|-------------|\n| `GET /competitions` | public | Competition hub |\n| `POST /competitions/api/json` | public | Published competition discovery |\n| `GET /competitions/<edition-slug>` | public | Competition overview with published divisions |\n| `GET /competitions/<edition-slug>/format` | public | Published format and bracket information |\n| `GET /competitions/<edition-slug>/schedule` | public | Published competition schedule |\n| `GET /competitions/<edition-slug>/gamedays/<matchday-id>` | public | Published game-day board |\n| `GET /competitions/<edition-slug>/register?division_id=<division-id>` | user | Club registration form |\n| `POST /competitions/<edition-slug>/register?division_id=<division-id>` | user | Club registration submission |\n| `GET /competitions/<edition-slug>/divisions/<division-slug>/schedule.ics` | public | Division calendar export |\n| `GET /api/v1/competitions` | public | Published competition API index |\n| `GET /api/v1/competitions/<edition-slug>` | public | Complete published competition feed |\n| `GET /teams/<slug>` | public | Public team profile |\n\nThere are no tournament-named or numeric compatibility routes. The product had not been released when the namespace was consolidated, so only `/competitions` is supported.\n\n### `PublicFollowController`\n\nCanonical follow and discovery routes:\n\n| Route | Auth | Description |\n|-------|------|-------------|\n| `GET /seasons` | public | Season discovery page for published seasons |\n| `GET /seasons/<slug>` | public | Canonical season landing page with editorial and tournament sections |\n| `GET /teams/<slug>/schedule` | public | Team-centric grouped upcoming schedule |\n| `GET /teams/<slug>/results` | public | Team-centric recent approved results |\n| `GET /teams/<slug>/schedule.ics` | public | Team schedule calendar export |\n| `GET /api/v1/teams/<slug>/feed` | public | Stable v1 team follow feed |\n\n## Public-site behaviour\n\n1. Tournament-first navigation: the visible website now centers tournaments and nests published coverage under the existing Tournaments area.\n2. Slug-first URLs: tournaments and teams use canonical slug routes, with numeric routes preserved only for backward compatibility.\n3. Editorial control: operators can feature tournaments, add summaries, pin announcements, and assign hero imagery without editing templates.\n4. Better hub discovery: the main hub separates featured, live, recent, and archived tournaments instead of showing one flat list.\n5. Team-aware public pages: standings, results, schedule, and teams pages link through to public team profiles when available.\n6. Sticky section navigation: tournament sub-pages share one consistent local navigation model.\n7. Calendar export: schedules can be consumed as `.ics` for personal or club calendars.\n8. Stable feed contract: the v1 feed includes slug-aware public URLs alongside tournament, standings, schedule, bracket, and result data.\n9. Visibility enforcement: results and standings respect per-tournament publication toggles.\n10. Sanitized website rendering: public descriptions and website content render through standard website field handling.\n11. Backward compatibility: legacy competition routes keep resolving while the visible product language is tournament-first.\n12. Season discovery and editorial planning: published seasons aggregate featured tournaments, recent tournaments, and live editorial items with publication windows.\n13. Automatic menu cleanup: module installs and upgrades normalize stale `/competitions` website menus into the Tournaments submenu and remove leftover duplicates.\n14. Shared privileged-write boundary: website registration POST routes validate HTTP input and then reuse the same tournament registration helper as the portal layer.\n15. Registration feedback redirects preserve the division selector query parameter, so capacity and validation messages return to the correct registration form.\n16. Publication handoff guidance: tournament and standings Website tabs now explain whether visibility is blocked by tournament publication, missing standings, unpublished standings, or disabled standings toggles, with direct links back to standings when operators need to act there.\n\n## Publication guards\n\n- Controllers resolve tournaments, seasons, and team public routes through publication- and visibility-scoped queries before canonical redirects, page rendering, feed reads, or registration handling.\n- Unpublished tournaments lose public access immediately, regardless of related data still present in the database.\n- Legacy numeric compatibility routes redirect only while the tournament still satisfies the relevant public visibility guard.\n- Season slug and numeric detail routes only resolve website-published seasons.\n- Team profile, schedule, results, ICS, and feed routes only resolve teams with a published competition footprint.\n- Public results are limited to approved results.\n- Public participant lists exclude withdrawn entries.\n- Standings exposure depends on both tournament publication and the standings visibility controls.\n\n\n## Public controller boundaries\n\nRoute orchestration lives in `public_competitions.py`. Framework response, canonical resolution, authenticated club lookup, error redirect, and rate-limit response behavior live in `_public_request.py`. New domain queries belong in model query services rather than either controller helper.\n\n\n## Complete competition API\n\n`GET /api/v1/competitions` lists published competitions; `GET /api/v1/competitions/<edition-slug>` aggregates every published division and its public teams, schedules, approved results, standings, brackets, and links.\n\n## Competition route upgrade safety\n\nThe `19.0.3.0.0` pre-migration repairs the stored selector of the competition-hub editorial extension before Odoo reloads its parent template. This prevents databases upgraded from the former route namespace from failing registry initialization while the canonical `/competitions` templates are loaded. Fresh installations already use the canonical selector and require no special action.\n\n## Public schedule presentation\n\nPublic game-day schedules render stored UTC slot datetimes in the federation\ncompany timezone before grouping and formatting them. Match cards focus on the\ncourt and participating teams. Internal publication labels, stage names, and\nround numbers are hidden for normal published matches; exceptional live statuses\nsuch as delayed, moved, postponed, or cancelled remain visible.\n\n", "content_length": 10559, "filesystem_mode": "0644", "git_mode": "100644", "git_state": "tracked", "path": "sports_federation_public_site/README.md", "record_type": "file", "sha256": "20224111ca5eb5f9f0ede27e085867cc1ba987080963998672aa564bc6aa884c", "size_bytes": 10559}

 {"content": "\"\"\"Tour T-09: Public Site — Tournament Publication and Editorial Scheduling\n\nWalks the public site publication workflow:\n  1. Create a tournament and publish it (website_published = True)\n  2. Create an editorial item in draft\n  3. Schedule the item → 'scheduled'\n  4. Publish the item → 'published'\n  5. Archive the item → 'archived'\n  6. Reset to draft → 'draft'\n  7. Verify get_live_items() returns only published/in-window items\n\nKey invariants verified:\n- editorial item states: draft → scheduled → published → archived → draft\n- get_live_items() excludes draft and archived items\n- tournament website_published field is settable\n\"\"\"\n\nfrom odoo.tests.common import TransactionCase\n\n\nclass TestTourPublication(TransactionCase):\n    \"\"\"T-09: Public site publication lifecycle tour.\"\"\"\n\n    @classmethod\n    def setUpClass(cls):\n        super().setUpClass()\n\n        cls.season = cls.env[\"federation.season\"].create(\n            {\n                \"name\": \"Publication Tour Season\",\n                \"code\": \"PUB26\",\n                \"date_start\": \"2026-01-01\",\n                \"date_end\": \"2026-12-31\",\n            }\n        )\n        cls.tournament = cls.env[\"federation.tournament\"].create(\n            {\n                \"name\": \"Publication Tour Cup\",\n                \"season_id\": cls.season.id,\n                \"date_start\": \"2026-06-01\",\n            }\n        )\n\n    def test_publication_lifecycle(self):\n        \"\"\"Tournament publication + editorial item state machine.\"\"\"\n\n        # STEP 1: Publish tournament\n        self.assertFalse(self.tournament.website_published)\n        self.tournament.website_published = True\n        self.assertTrue(self.tournament.website_published)\n\n        # STEP 2: Create an editorial item in draft\n        item = self.env[\"federation.public.editorial.item\"].create(\n            {\n                \"name\": \"Tour Launch Announcement\",\n                \"summary\": \"The Tour Cup 2026 is now open!\",\n                \"content_type\": \"announcement\",\n                \"publish_start\": \"2026-01-01 09:00:00\",\n                \"season_id\": self.season.id,\n                \"tournament_id\": self.tournament.id,\n            }\n        )\n        self.assertEqual(item.publication_state, \"draft\")\n        self.assertFalse(item.can_access_publicly())\n\n        # STEP 3: Schedule the item\n        item.action_schedule()\n        self.assertEqual(item.publication_state, \"scheduled\")\n\n        # STEP 4: Publish\n        item.action_publish()\n        self.assertEqual(item.publication_state, \"published\")\n        self.assertTrue(item.can_access_publicly())\n\n        # STEP 5: get_live_items returns published item\n        live = self.env[\"federation.public.editorial.item\"].get_live_items(\n            season=self.season\n        )\n        self.assertIn(item, live)\n\n        # STEP 6: Archive — removed from live feed\n        item.action_archive_item()\n        self.assertEqual(item.publication_state, \"archived\")\n        self.assertFalse(item.can_access_publicly())\n\n        live_after_archive = self.env[\n            \"federation.public.editorial.item\"\n        ].get_live_items(season=self.season)\n        self.assertNotIn(item, live_after_archive)\n\n        # STEP 7: Reset to draft\n        item.action_reset_to_draft()\n        self.assertEqual(item.publication_state, \"draft\")\n\n        # STEP 8: Second item — publish directly from draft (skip scheduled)\n        item2 = self.env[\"federation.public.editorial.item\"].create(\n            {\n                \"name\": \"Direct Publish Item\",\n                \"summary\": \"Published directly without scheduling.\",\n                \"content_type\": \"highlight\",\n                \"season_id\": self.season.id,\n            }\n        )\n        item2.action_publish()\n        self.assertEqual(item2.publication_state, \"published\")\n", "content_length": 3777, "filesystem_mode": "0644", "git_mode": "100644", "git_state": "tracked", "path": "sports_federation_public_site/tests/test_tour_publication.py", "record_type": "file", "sha256": "b7b1bd55a919ea2280a3fc6072bdd76d3947178833e5d679eed8e65465b10a37", "size_bytes": 3799}

+    def _division_label(self, match):
+        fixture = match.logical_fixture_id
+        division = fixture.division_id if fixture else False
+        if not division:
+            return False
+        label = division.name or division.display_name
+        gender_labels = {
+            "male": _("Men"),
+            "female": _("Women"),
+            "mixed": _("Mixed"),
+        }
+        gender = gender_labels.get(division.gender)
+        if gender and gender.lower() not in (label or "").lower():
+            return _("%(division)s · %(gender)s", division=label, gender=gender)
+        return label
